For sine same/different frequencies:

def softmax_logsumexp(y):
    y = y - np.max(y)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = e.sum()
    return e/s
```

Score function for coefficients:
```
def score_from_coeffs(coeffs, C, n, ffl):
    f = 1.0 + C @ coeffs
    np.maximum(f,0,out=f)
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return inf
    p=f/s
    c = conv_max_fft(p)
    return 2*n*c
```

def local_search(p0, budget_s, max_iter=None):
    n=len(p0); p0 normalized; y=log(p0) - max
    ffl=1; while ffl<3*n: ffl*=2 # for correlation maybe 2*? For conv p⋆p need Lp>=2n-1. For gradient corr need Lcorr>=3n-1? We can compute separately.
    best_y=y.copy(); best_score = exact_score(p0)
    lr = 0.01; tau_frac=0.5; beta1=0.9,beta2=0.999
    t=0
    while time<deadline and t<max_iter:
       p=softmax(y)
       c=convolution_fft(p, ffl_conv) # length 2n-1
       maxc=c.max()
       # use smooth weights with frac current: frac = max(0.01, tau_frac * 0.98^t)
       w = softmax((c-maxc)/(frac*maxc))
       grad_p = gradient_corr(w,p, ffl_corr)
       grad_y = grad_p - p*np.dot(p, grad_p)
       # Adam update with backtracking maybe
       y_new = adam_update(y,grad_y,lr,state)
       y_new -= y_new.mean()
       p_new=softmax(y_new)
       score_new = exact_score_fft(p_new) # recompute c maybe already? Could use c from gradient? For score after update need new c.
       if score_new + 1e-12 < best_score:
           best_score=score_new; best_y=y_new; best_p=p_new
           y=y_new; improve=True; maybe lr*=1.05
       else:
           # try smaller lr up to 5 times or accept with no? To maintain progress, if no accept decrease lr.
```

def local_search_log(p0, budget_s, n, step_lr=0.02, tau_frac=0.1):
    p = p0.copy()/p0.sum()
    y = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    y -= y.max()
    ffl_conv = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    ffl_corr = next_pow2(3*n-1) maybe for gradient.
    state_m, state_v, t=...
    best_score = score_fft(p); best_y = y.copy(); best_p = p.copy()
    no_improve=0
    lr = step_lr
    frac = tau_frac
    while time...:
        c=convolve_fft(p)
        maxc=c.max()
        # smooth weights
        if maxc<=0: break
        # frac anneal: start 0.5 to 0.02 based on fraction of budget use? Could set frac = max(0.02, 0.5 * decay^iter)
        w = softmax_shifted(c, frac*maxc)
        grad_p = gradient_from_weights(w)
        grad_y = grad_p - p*np.dot(p, grad_p)
        # Adam update
        t +=1; m=...; v=...
        y_new = y - lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        y_new -= y_new.mean()
        p_new = softmax(y_new)
        score_new = score_fft(p_new)
        if score_new + 1e-12 < best_score:
           best...
           # optionally keep y_new; update y
        else:
           no_improve++; if no_improve>50: lr *=0.7; frac *=0.8? no_improve=0; maybe add noise
        # add small random perturbation occasionally to escape?
    return best_p,best_score
```

```
def convolve_square(p, ffl):
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, ffl)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, ffl)[:2*p.size-1]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```

Need maybe implement `next_pow2(x)`.
```
def next_power_of_two(x):
    x=max(1,int(x)); return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

`project_simplex(v)`:
Standard. Need efficient. Implementation:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    if n==1: return np.zeros_like(v) + v.sum()
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1))-cssum>0)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssum[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
```
But if v.sum() not 1, project to sum=1. For general sum S, formula with target S. Use target. If we want preserve sum approx, use target = v.sum()? For p sum1, target1. In projected gradient, we project to sum1. Implement `project_to_simplex(v, target=1.0)`.
```
def project_to_simplex(v, target=1.0):
   if target<=0: return zeros
   u=sort(v)[::-1]
   cssum=cumsum(u)
   cond = u*(arange(1,n+1)) - cssum > target
   rho = max np.flatnonzero(cond)[-1]
   theta = (cssum[rho]-target)/(rho+1)
   return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
If no cond? For target>0, at least? If v all very negative? Then cond maybe none; rho=0? Need handle. Use:
```
rho = np.searchsorted? Standard:
  tmp = u*(1:n) - cssum
  rho = max(i+1 such that tmp[i]>0) for target=1.
```
If none, set rho=n-1? Then theta=cssum[n-1]-target over n maybe negative; max returns v-theta >0. Works.
```
idx = np.arange(1,n+1)
rho = (u*idx - cssum > target).nonzero()[0]
if rho.size==0: rho=n-1 else rho=rho[-1]
```
Okay.

m=c.max()

`gradient_weights(w, p, ffl, n)` using fp? We can compute inside local loop to reuse. Top-level for standalone:
```
def gradient_from_weights(w, p, ffl):
   n=p.size
   w_rev=w[::-1]
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, ffl)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p, ffl)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(fw*fp, ffl)
   idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
   grad = 2.0*corr[idx]
   # due to float, maybe small; return grad
```
But if using same ffl as score, need ensure ffl>=3n-2. In local, set ffl=next_power_of_two(3*n-1).

def coefficient_objective(x):
    C = _COEFF_STATE['C']; n=...
    v = C @ x
    f = 1.0 + v
    if f.min() < 0: f -= f.min()
    s=f.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return np.inf
    p=f/s
    maxc,_=conv_max_fft(p, _COEFF_STATE['ffl'])
    return 2*n*maxc
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_seq = None; best_score = np.inf

```

```
def random_coeff(rng, M, shift_seed):
   d=2*M
   coeff=np.zeros(d)
   if rng.random()<0.25:
       # known
       coeff[0]=rng.normal(0.05,0.05)
       coeff[2]=rng.normal(-0.72,0.05)
       # maybe c3
       coeff[4]=rng.normal(0.0,0.15)
       # random small cos
       coeff[1]=rng.normal(0,0.05)
       coeff[3]=rng.normal(0,0.05)
       coeff[5]=rng.normal(0,0.05)
       coeff[7]=rng.normal(0,0.05)
   else:
       amp=[0.9,0.8,0.5,0.3]
       for m: amp_m=amp[min(m,len-1)]*rng.uniform(0.5,1.0)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       coeff[2*m]=amp_m*np.cos(phase) # cos?
       coeff[2*m+1]=amp_m*np.sin(phase)
   return coeff
```
But assigning cosine phase means coefficient vector amplitude r, phase where f=1+r cos(ωx+φ)= r cosφ cos + -r sinφ sin. If basis [sin,cos], coeff_cos=r cosφ, coeff_sin=-r sinφ. Doesn't matter.

def coeffs_to_sequence(coeff, n, M, shift):
   i=(np.arange(n)+shift)/float(n)
   v = C_high @ coeff
   f=1+v
   if f.min()<0: f-=f.min()
   if f.sum()<=0: f=ones
   p=f/f.sum()
   # optional zero floor? np.maximum(p,0)
   return p
```

`random_trig_search` implementation details for top list:
```
def trig_random_search(n, M, shift, trials, rng, ffl):
   C=make_basis(n,M,shift)
   top=[]
   for i in range(trials):
      coeff=sample_coeff(rng,M,known=True)
      f=1+C@coeff; f=np.maximum(f,0) maybe f = f - min(f,0)
      s=f.sum()
      if s<=... continue
      p=f/s
      maxc,_=conv_max_fft(p,ffl)
      score=2*n*maxc
      if len(top)<10: insert sorted
      else if score<top[-1].score: replace
   return top sorted
```

Local search details code:
```
def local_search(p0, deadline, rng, alpha0=0.25, focus0=0.15, label=""):
    n = p0.size
    p = np.asarray(p0,float).copy(); p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return...
    p /= s
    ffl = next_power_of_two(3*n - 2)  # for score and grad. For n maybe 2048 =>8192? 3n-2=6142 next8192. n=4096 =>12286 next16384.
    maxc, c = score_and_c(p, ffl) ? Need score.
    current_score = 2*n*maxc
    best_score = current_score; best_p=p.copy()
    alpha = alpha0
    focus = focus0
    no_improve=0; iter=0
    while time < deadline:
       # recompute c if p changed? current c from last score? We can store c from last accepted p. After accepted p, we have c from line? We may not store. Simpler compute at start of each loop.
       c, maxc, fp = conv_and_fp(p, ffl)
       current_score = 2*n*maxc
       if current_score < best_score - 1e-12: best... (shouldn't)
       if maxc <= 1/(2*n) * 1.0001 maybe lower bound reached; but target not.
       # weights
       sigma = focus * maxc
       if sigma<=0: sigma=1e-12
       z = (c - maxc) / sigma
       # To avoid underflow, z max0
       w = np.exp(z)
       wsum = w.sum()
       if not finite or wsum==0: w = np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
       else w/=wsum
       # gradient
       w_rev = w[::-1]
       fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, ffl)
       corr = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, ffl)
       idx = 2*n - 2 - np.arange(n)
       grad = 2.0 * corr[idx]
       # clean grad
       grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0)
       d = grad.float.copy()
       # Remove mean to preserve sum? We will project, but remove small.
       d -= np.mean(d)
       dnorm = np.linalg.norm(d)
       if dnorm <= 1e-15:
           # perturb p
           p = project_to_simplex(p + rng.normal(0, 1e-6/n, n), 1.0)
           continue
       pnorm = np.linalg.norm(p)
       step = alpha * pnorm / dnorm
       # To avoid too large, cap step*d norm
       accepted=False
       try_steps = [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*1.5?]
       # Try increasing? Maybe if no improve, try slightly larger than step? Use [step*1.5, step, 0.7,0.5,0.2]
       for scale in (1.5,1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15):
           q = p - step*scale*d
           q = project_to_simplex(q, 1.0)
           qmax, _ = conv_max_fft(q, ffl)
           sc = 2*n*qmax
           if sc < current_score - 1e-15*max(1,current_score):
              p = q; current_score=sc;
              if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy(); }
              accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
          no_improve=0
          # if big improvement, maybe increase alpha slightly
          alpha = min(2.0, alpha*1.05)
          focus = min(0.3, max(0.03, focus*0.99))  # slightly focus
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          alpha = max(0.02, alpha*0.8)
          focus = max(0.02, focus*0.9)  # more focused? Or increase? If stuck, maybe broader
          if no_improve % 25 ==0:
             # random perturbation
             noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
             noise -= noise.mean()
             scale = 0.02 * np.linalg.norm(p) / np.sqrt(n)
             p = project_to_simplex(p + scale*noise, 1.0)
             current_score = 2*n*conv_max_fft(p, ffl)[0]
             if current_score > best_score:
                 p=best_p.copy(); current_score=best_score
       iter +=1
       if iter % 200 ==0: print progress
   return best_p,best_score
```

Interpolation high-n from low-n:
```
def upsample_distribution(p_small, n_large):
   # Use cumulative CDF: small positions i/(ns). We want n_large distribution approximating same density. Could simply linearly interpolate p as function of x.
   xs_small = np.linspace(0,1,ns,endpoint=False) + 0.5/ns? maybe.
   xs_large = np.linspace(0,1,nlarge,endpoint=False)+0.5/nlarge
   p_large = np.interp(xs_large, xs_small, p_small*np.ones? Actually p values density)
   p_large /= sum
```
This smooths. Or use block repeat and smoothing: p_small repeated `n_large/ns`, then convolve with kernel width maybe 2 to smooth. But if n_large not exact multiple, use tile and truncate. Since p_small is discrete sequence optimized; repeating exactly may create periodic copies; high-n score factor 4 times n, max convolution? If p_large = p_small repeated r times and normalized (sum r), the convolution has supports; max might be r? Let's compute: If p_small sum1, repeated r blocks psize sum r. Normalize /r. Max convolution of repeated blocks? The sum distribution of two iid copies has two components: block index sum and within. The within-block convolution max ≤ max_small. For block indices, there are r block positions; if p_small concentrated? Could increase max by factor? For r=4, central block-index peak maybe mass of same indices? For each within pair, block sum distribution; if p_small has flat histogram over blocks? Hard. Might not be good. Interpolation better.
But if p_small optimized length 1024, a direct high-n via coefficient better.

```
def upsample_p(p, n_large):
    ns=p.size
    if ns==n_large: return p.copy()
    if n_large < ns: ... average blocks?
    xs_s=(np.arange(ns)+0.5)/ns
    xs_l=(np.arange(n_large)+0.5)/n_large
    # Interpolate p as values; but p sum changes; use p as density height? If xs_l not matching, `np.interp` returns values with same scale; sum~1. Normalize.
    p_large=np.interp(xs_l, xs_s, p, left=p[0], right=p[-1])
    p_large/=p_large.sum()
    return p_large
```
This might blur but okay.

`def make_trig_basis(n, M, shift):`
```
i = (np.arange(n) + float(shift)) / float(n)
cols=[np.ones(n)]? Wait coefficient vector no constant. If we call `v = C @ x` where x osc only. Basis columns just trig. No constant. Use no constant.
for m in range(1,M+1):
   ang = 2*np.pi*m*i
   cols.append(np.sin(ang))
   cols.append(np.cos(ang))
return np.column_stack(cols)
```
If using coefficient vector [sin1, cos1, sin2, cos2,...]. Good.

`def coeff_to_p(x, n, M, shift, C=None):`
If C None, make. But for speed pass C.
```
v = C @ x
f = 1.0 + v
mn = f.min()
if mn < 0: f = f - mn
s = f.sum()
if s <= 1e-300: return np.ones(n)/n
p = f/s
p = np.maximum(p,0)
s=p.sum()
if s<=0: uniform
p/=s
return p
```
Using f=min shift. Note if mn negative small, subtract. Good.

`def coeff_score(x, C, n, ffl):`

...
```
For global state, M not needed.

`def coord_search_coeff(x0, C, n, ffl, deadline, rng):`
Implement top-level without accessing state. It can call `coeff_score` directly.
Pseudo:
```
x=x0.astype(float).copy(); current=coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl)
step=np.full(x.size,0.1)
no_pass=0
while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    improved=False
    for j in range(x.size):
        best_j=x[j]; best_sc=current
        for sgn in (1,-1):
            for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
                x[j]=best_j + sgn*step[j]*mult
                sc=coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc=sc; best_j=x[j]
        if best_sc < current -1e-12:
            x[j]=best_j; current=best_sc; improved=True
        else:
            x[j]=best_j
    if improved:
       no_pass=0
       step=np.minimum(0.5, step*1.1)
    else:
       no_pass+=1
       step*=0.7
       if np.max(step) < 1e-5: break
       # random kick
       j=rng.integers(0,x.size); x[j]+=rng.normal(0,0.05)
       sc=coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl)
       if sc > current + 1e-8: x=x0? maybe revert to best?
```
Need store best x. Simpler keep `best_x`, `best_score`; if improved update. If coordinate pass no improve, reduce step and maybe random from best_x.
```
best_x=x.copy(); best_score=current
...

   x=best_x.copy(); step*=0.7; if max step <1e-5 break; x[j]+=rng.normal(0,0.05); sc=...; if sc<best_score update else maybe keep? 
```

    for m in range(M):
       amax=amp[min(m,len(amp)-1)]
       r=rng.uniform(0.0, amax)

       # coefficient for sin/cos = r*[cos?], use [sin, cos]

       x[2*m+1]=r*np.sin(th)
    # sometimes zero one component to near sine?
return x
```

Now, let's write `conv_and_fp` top-level:
```
def conv_and_fp(p, ffl):
    n=p.size
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p, ffl)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, ffl)[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c, float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0, fp
```

`score_fft_only(p, ffl)` calls conv_and_fp but discards. To avoid recompute fp? fine.

`project_to_simplex`:
```
def project_to_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if target <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    mask = u*idx - cssum > float(target)
    if np.any(mask):
       rho = int(np.flatnonzero(mask)[-1])
    else:
       rho = n-1
    theta = (cssum[rho] - float(target)) / (rho+1.0)
    w = v - theta
    np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
    s = w.sum()
    if s > 0:
       w /= (s/float(target)) # if target not 1
    # to avoid sum error due clipping, could re-project? This scaling may violate nonneg? no.
    return w
```
If target=1, w.sum should target. Scaling fine. If s=0, return uniform target/n? But target 1. Use:
```
if s <= 0: w = np.full(n, target/n); return w
```
Actually if all v very negative, mask none rho=n-1 theta negative? Then w positive; s>0.

Could use `np.sort` each line trial; okay.

`upsample_p`:
```
def upsample_p(p, n_large):
    p = np.asarray(p,float); ns=p.size
    if ns == n_large: return p/p.sum()
    if n_large < 1: return p
    xs_s = (np.arange(ns)+0.5)/float(ns)
    xs_l = (np.arange(n_large)+0.5)/float(n_large)
    if ns ==1:
       p_large = np.full(n_large, p[0])
    else:
       p_large = np.interp(xs_l, xs_s, p, left=p[0], right=p[-1])
    p_large = np.maximum(p_large, 0)
    s=p_large.sum()
    if s<=0: p_large=np.full(n_large,1.0/n_large)
    else: p_large/=s
    return p_large
```
If n_large not much bigger, okay. If n_large < ns, downsampling? Use average blocks? Could implement but likely not needed. If ns > n_large, simple reshape if multiple else interp with xs reversed? We can just for general:
```
if ns > n_large:
   # block average by linear interpolation in reverse? use np.interp(p as values? Better: sum blocks.
   p_large = np.zeros(n_large)
   for i in range(n_large):
      lo = int(i*ns/n_large); hi=max(lo+1,int((i+1)*ns/n_large)); p_large[i]=p[lo:hi].sum()
   normalize
```
But top-level loop n_large maybe 2048, okay. Simpler use interpolation even if ns>n_large: `np.interp(xs_l, xs_s, p)` works (decreasing sample? xs_l in same range, no matter). It will downsample approximately. fine.

`coord_search_coeff`: Need careful with time; if x improvements, continue. Let's write:
```
def coord_search_coeff(x0, C, n, ffl, deadline, rng):
    x=x0.copy(); current=coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl); best_x=x.copy(); best_score=current
    step=np.full(x.size,0.08)
    no_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       improved=False
       for j in range(x.size):
          base=x[j]; best_j=base; best_sc=current
          for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
             for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
                x[j]=base + sign*step[j]*mult
                sc=coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl)
                if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                   best_sc=sc; best_j=x[j]
          if best_sc < current - 1e-10:
             x[j]=best_j; current=best_sc; improved=True
             if current < best_score:
                best_score=current; best_x=x.copy()
          else:
             x[j]=base
       no_improve += 0 if improved else 1
       if improved:
          no_improve=0
          step=np.minimum(0.25, step*1.05)
       else:
          step *= 0.75
          if np.max(step) < 1e-5:
             break
          if no_improve % 10 == 0:
             # random kick from best
             x=best_x.copy()
             j=int(rng.integers(0,x.size))
             x[j] += float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05))
             sc=coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl)
             if sc < best_score:
                best_score=sc; best_x=x.copy(); current=sc
             # if not, current=best_score? set x=best.
             x=best_x.copy(); current=best_score
    return best_x,best_score
```
This coordinate search may be slow: For each j, 6 evals; d=8 =>48 evals/pass. 100 passes 4800 evals. Time maybe at n=1024 ~5s? okay.
But if scipy not available with 120s each candidate, could be too much. We rely on scipy maybe. If not, coordinate okay.

Could optimize coordinate by trying only ±full step then half; okay.

Loop:
```
iters=0
while time < deadline:
   if iters and iters%100==0: print(label, current_score, best_score)
   c,maxc,fp = conv_and_fp(p, ffl)
   s=p.sum(); current_score=2*n*maxc/(s*s)
   if not finite: p=best_p; current_score=best_score
   if current_score + 1e-15 < best_score: update best
   if maxc <= 0: break
   sigma = focus * maxc
   if sigma <= 1e-300: sigma=1e-12
   z=(c - maxc)/sigma
   # To avoid all underflow, set z[np.isposinf?]
   w=np.exp(z)
   wsum=w.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <=0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
   else: w/=wsum
   w_rev=w[::-1]
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, ffl)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, ffl)
   idx=2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
   grad=2.0*corr[idx]
   grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
   d=grad - grad.mean()
   dnorm=np.linalg.norm(d)
   pnorm=np.linalg.norm(p)
   if dnorm <= 1e-15 or pnorm <=0:
       p = project_to_simplex(p + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-6/n, n), 1.0)
       continue
   base = alpha * pnorm / dnorm
   # line search scales
   accepted=False
   for scl in (1.6, 1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15):
       q = p - base*scl*d
       q = project_to_simplex(q, 1.0)
       sc = score_fft(q, ffl)
       if sc < current_score - 1e-12:
           p = q; current_score = sc; accepted=True
           break
   if accepted:
       if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score = current_score; best_p=p.copy()
       no_improve=0
       alpha = min(1.5, alpha * 1.05)
       focus = max(0.02, focus*0.995)
   else:
       no_improve +=1
       alpha = max(0.05, alpha*0.85)
       focus = max(0.02, focus*0.9)
       if no_improve > 100:
          p = project_to_simplex(best_p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02*best_p.std()+1e-6/n, n), 1.0)
          current_score = score_fft(p, ffl)
          if current_score >= best_score:
             p = best_p.copy(); current_score = best_score
          no_improve=0
          alpha = alpha0; focus = focus0
   iters +=1
return best_p,best_score
```
`best_p.std()` maybe if all zeros? positive. If noise std too high, `0.02*std` okay. For p std ~1/√n ~0.02 for n=2048? Actually std of p around 0.0005? Maybe 0.05? Noise 0.02*0.0005=1e-5, okay. `score_fft` robust:
```
def score_fft(p, ffl=None):
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.inf
   if ffl is None: ffl=next_power_of_two(2*p.size-1)
   mc=conv_max_fft(p, ffl)[0]
   return 2.0*p.size*mc/(s*s)
```
This recomputes rfft; in local line trial, p is already normalized, sum1. Could skip sum for speed, but robust. For line trial many times, sum O(n) negligible. Use a faster `score_fft_norm(p, ffl)` assuming sum1? Maybe not necessary. But local hot path: line trial 6* sum O(n) negligible compared FFT.

In local current score, after `conv_and_fp`, p sum maybe 1. Use robust.

Now, maybe `np.exp(z)` with z could include NaN if maxc zero; handled. If c contains negative tiny, z maybe positive for negative? max is max; negative less; z negative. good.

Now, if `focus` is too large, sigma large, weights uniform-ish. okay.

Potential issue: `corr` from FFT has length ffl, but due cyclic wrap, if ffl <2n+wlen? We set ffl=next_power_of_two(3*n-2). For n=1? We use large. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_origin = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 40.0)

amp = [0.8, 0.9, 0.55, 0.4, 0.25]
if rng.random() < 0.35:
   x = np.zeros(2*M)
   x[0] = rng.normal(0.086,0.08)
   if M>=2: x[2] = rng.normal(-0.72,0.08)
   if M>=3: x[4] = rng.normal(0.0,0.12)
   if M>=4: x[6] = rng.normal(0.0,0.08)
   for k in range(1,2*M,2): x[k] = rng.normal(0.0,0.05)
else:
   x=np.zeros(2*M)
   for m in range(M):
      amax=amp[min(m,len(amp)-1)]
      r=rng.uniform(0.0, amax)
      th=rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
      x[2*m] = r*np.cos(th)
      x[2*m+1] = r*np.sin(th)
   # sometimes enforce second harmonic dominant
   if rng.random()<0.3 and M>=2:
      r=rng.uniform(0.3,0.9); th=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      x[2]=r*np.cos(th); x[3]=r*np.sin(th)
return x
```

```
def refine_top_coefficients(top, n, M, deadline, rng):
    out=[]
    if not top: return out
    k = min(3, len(top))
    slack = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    per = slack / k if k>0 else 0
    for i in range(k):
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       score,item=top[i]
       shift,x=item
       C=make_trig_basis(n,M,shift)
       ffl=next_power_of_two(3*n-1)
       dl = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + per*1.2)
       xr, scr = optimize_coefficient(x, C, n, ffl, dl, rng)
       add_top(out, scr, (shift, xr), 8)
    for score,value in top:
       add_top(out, score, value, 8)
    out.sort(key=_score_key)
    return out
```

`coeff_score` top-level:
```
def coeff_score(x,C,n,ffl):
    p=coeff_to_p(x,n,C)
    mc,_=conv_max_fft(p,ffl)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*mc/(s*s)
```

Now, if `coeff_to_p` shifts when min negative, it changes effective offset. For large coefficients, many templates may shift; objective may prefer shift. okay.

Let's ensure `coefficient_score_state` if `x` length mismatch due Nelder? It returns inf if C @ x size mismatch? `C @ x` raises ValueError. Wrap in try.
```
try: p=coeff_to_p...
except Exception: return np.inf
```
Good.

`coeff_score` also try? Could.
```
try: ... except: return np.inf
```

`conv_max_fft` if `ffl` smaller than 2n-1? We pass. But if p size 0? no.

Now, final code. We'll include `#` comments minimal. Ensure no lambda. Let's produce.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    score = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

by combining:
  - low-dimensional trigonometric ansatz p_i = 1 + sum_k A_k sin(2 pi k i/n)
    with optional phase and clipping to enforce non-negativity,
  - small-scale random/coordinate/Nelder-Mead coefficient refinement,
  - full coordinate projection-based local search on the simplex.

The final program returns the best sequence found before the time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
except Exception:
    _scipy_opt = None

_COEFF_STATE = {"C": None, "n": 0, "ffl": 1}


def _score_key(t):
    return t[0]


def next_power_of_two(x):
    x = max(1, int(x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def conv_max_fft(p, ffl=None):
    """Return (max convolution coefficient, coefficients) for p*p (full)."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=float)
    if ffl is None:
        ffl = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, ffl)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, ffl)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, c
    return float(c.max()), c


def score_fft(p, ffl=None):
    """Fast score approximation using FFT convolution."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n = p.size
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if n == 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    mc, _ = conv_max_fft(p, ffl)
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def conv_and_fp(p, ffl):
    """Compute c, max(c), and rfft(p) using the same transform length."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    n = p.size
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, ffl)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, ffl)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c, 0.0, fp
    return c, float(c.max()), fp


def normalize_sequence(p):
    """Non-negative normalization to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=float)
    return p / s


def project_to_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    """Euclidean projection of v onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=target}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    target = float(target)
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    mask = (u * idx - cssum) > target
    if np.any(mask):
        rho = int(np.flatnonzero(mask)[-1])
    else:
        rho = n - 1
    theta = (float(cssum[rho]) - target) / float(rho + 1)
    w = v - theta
    np.maximum(w, 0.0, out=w)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(n, target / n, dtype=float)
    else:
        w *= target / s
    return w


def upsample_p(p, n_large):
    """Approximate resampling of a probability sequence to length n_large."""
    p = normalize_sequence(p)
    ns = p.size
    n_large = int(n_large)
    if ns == n_large:
        return p.copy()
    if n_large <= 0:
        return p.copy()
    xs_s = (np.arange(ns) + 0.5) / float(ns)
    xs_l = (np.arange(n_large) + 0.5) / float(n_large)
    if ns == 1:
        q = np.full(n_large, float(p[0]), dtype=float)
    else:
        q = np.interp(xs_l, xs_s, p, left=float(p[0]), right=float(p[-1]))
    return normalize_sequence(q)


def make_trig_basis(n, M, shift):
    """Basis columns [sin(2*pi*1*i/n), cos(...), sin(2*pi*2*i/n), ...]."""
    i = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + float(shift)) / float(n)
    cols = []
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
        cols.append(np.cos(ang))
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def coeff_to_p(x, n, C):
    """Convert trigonometric coefficient vector x to a non-negative probability p."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    try:
        v = C @ x
        f = 1.0 + v
        mn = float(np.min(f))
        if mn < 0.0:
            f = f - mn
        f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
        p = f / float(np.sum(f))
    except Exception:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=float)
    return normalize_sequence(p)


def coeff_score(x, C, n, ffl):
    """Score of trigonometric coefficient vector."""
    try:
        p = coeff_to_p(x, n, C)
        mc, _ = conv_max_fft(p, ffl)
        return float(2.0 * n * mc)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def coefficient_score_state(x):
    """Objective for scipy; context is stored in _COEFF_STATE."""
    st = _COEFF_STATE
    if st["C"] is None:
        return np.inf
    try:
        p = coeff_to_p(x, st["n"], st["C"])
        mc, _ = conv_max_fft(p, st["ffl"])
        return float(2.0 * st["n"] * mc)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def deterministic_coeff_templates(M):
    """Hand-guided nearby templates, especially the strong second-harmonic family."""
    d = 2 * M
    res = []
    for a in (0.0, 0.05, 0.086, 0.12):
        for b in (-0.55, -0.65, -0.72, -0.80):
            x = np.zeros(d, dtype=float)
            x[0] = float(a)
            if M >= 2:
                x[2] = float(b)
            res.append(x)
            for c in (-0.12, 0.0, 0.12):
                y = np.zeros(d, dtype=float)
                y[0] = float(a)
                if M >= 2:
                    y[2] = float(b)
                if M >= 3:
                    y[4] = float(c)
                res.append(y)
    for e in (-0.06, 0.0, 0.06):
        y = np.zeros(d, dtype=float)
        y[0] = 0.086
        if M >= 2:
            y[2] = -0.72
        if M >= 4:
            y[6] = float(e)
        res.append(y)
    return res


def random_coefficients(rng, M):
    d = 2 * M
    x = np.zeros(d, dtype=float)
    if rng.random() < 0.38:
        x[0] = float(rng.normal(0.086, 0.08))
        if M >= 2:
            x[2] = float(rng.normal(-0.72, 0.08))
        if M >= 3:
            x[4] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.12))
        if M >= 4:
            x[6] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08))
        for k in range(1, d, 2):
            x[k] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05))
    else:
        amps = (0.80, 0.90, 0.55, 0.40, 0.25)
        for m in range(M):
            amax = float(amps[min(m, len(amps) - 1)])
            r = float(rng.uniform(0.0, amax))
            th = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            x[2 * m] = r * np.cos(th)
            x[2 * m + 1] = r * np.sin(th)
        if M >= 2 and rng.random() < 0.35:
            r = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.90))
            th = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            x[2] = r * np.cos(th)
            x[3] = r * np.sin(th)
    return x


def add_top(top, score, item, max_size=8):
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if len(top) < max_size:
        top.append((float(score), item))
        top.sort(key=_score_key)
    else:
        worst_i = 0
        worst_s = top[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > worst_s:
                worst_s = top[i][0]
                worst_i = i
        if score < worst_s:
            top[worst_i] = (float(score), item)
            top.sort(key=_score_key)


def trig_random_search(n, M, shift, deadline, rng, top_size=8):
    """Random search in a low-dimensional nonzero-mean trigonometric family."""
    C = make_trig_basis(int(n), int(M), float(shift))
    ffl = next_power_of_two(2 * int(n) - 1)
    top = []

    for x in deterministic_coeff_templates(M):
        sc = coeff_score(x, C, int(n), ffl)
        add_top(top, sc, (float(shift), x.copy()), top_size)

    trials = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        x = random_coefficients(rng, M)
        sc = coeff_score(x, C, int(n), ffl)
        add_top(top, sc, (float(shift), x.copy()), top_size)
        trials += 1
        if trials % 5000 == 0:
            best = top[0][0] if top else np.inf
            print(f"coeff random n={n} shift={shift:.2f} trials={trials} best={best:.8f}")

    top.sort(key=_score_key)
    return top[:top_size]


def coord_search_coeff(x0, C, n, ffl, deadline, rng):
    """Simple adaptive coordinate refinement for trigonometric coefficients."""
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy()
    current = coeff_score(x, C, int(n), ffl)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = current
    step = np.full(x.size, 0.08, dtype=float)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        improved = False
        for j in range(x.size):
            base = float(x[j])
            best_j = base
            best_sc = current
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
                    x[j] = base + sign * step[j] * mult
                    sc = coeff_score(x, C, int(n), ffl)
                    if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_j = float(x[j])
            if best_sc < current - 1e-10:
                x[j] = best_j
                current = best_sc
                improved = True
                if current < best_score:
                    best_score = current
                    best_x = x.copy()
            else:
                x[j] = base

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            step = np.minimum(0.25, step * 1.05)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            step *= 0.75
            if np.max(step) < 1e-5:
                break
            if no_improve % 10 == 0:
                x = best_x.copy()
                j = int(rng.integers(0, x.size))
                x[j] = float(x[j]) + float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05))
                sc = coeff_score(x, C, int(n), ffl)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_x = x.copy()
                    current = best_score
                x = best_x.copy()
                current = best_score

    return best_x, best_score


def optimize_coefficient(x0, C, n, ffl, deadline, rng):
    """Refine one coefficient vector using scipy if usable, plus coordinate search."""
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy()
    sc = coeff_score(x, C, int(n), ffl)
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = sc

    if _scipy_opt is not None and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 10.0:
        _COEFF_STATE["C"] = C
        _COEFF_STATE["n"] = int(n)
        _COEFF_STATE["ffl"] = int(ffl)
        maxiter = 220 if int(n) <= 1024 else 120
        try:
            res = _scipy_opt.minimize(
                coefficient_score_state,
                x.copy(),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-9},
            )
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
                xr = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                scr = coeff_score(xr, C, int(n), ffl)
                if scr < best_score:
                    best_score = scr
                    best_x = xr.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        xr, scr = coord_search_coeff(best_x, C, int(n), ffl, deadline, rng)
        if scr < best_score:
            best_x = xr
            best_score = scr

    return best_x, best_score


def refine_top_coefficients(top, n, M, deadline, rng, max_refine=3):
    """Refine the best low-n coefficient candidates at a larger n."""
    out = []
    if not top:
        return out

    k = min(max_refine, len(top))
    slack = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    per = slack / float(max(1, k))

    for i in range(k):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        _, item = top[i]
        shift, x = item
        C = make_trig_basis(int(n), int(M), float(shift))
        ffl = next_power_of_two(3 * int(n) - 2)
        dl = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + per * 1.25)
        xr, scr = optimize_coefficient(x, C, int(n), ffl, dl, rng)
        add_top(out, scr, (float(shift), xr), 8)

    for sc, item in top:
        add_top(out, sc, item, 8)

    out.sort(key=_score_key)
    return out[:8]


def local_search(p0, deadline, rng, label="local", alpha0=0.25, focus0=0.15):
    """
    Simplex-local search on full coefficient vector.

    Uses active-set likelihood from FFT convolution and projected line searches.
    """
    p = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(p0, dtype=float))
    n = p.size
    if n < 8:
        n = 64
        p = upsample_p(p0, n)
    ffl = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)

    c, maxc, fp = conv_and_fp(p, ffl)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    current_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (s * s))
    best_score = current_score
    best_p = p.copy()

    alpha = float(alpha0)
    focus = float(focus0)
    no_improve = 0
    iters = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c, maxc, fp = conv_and_fp(p, ffl)
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        current_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc / (s * s)) if s > 0 else np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            p = best_p.copy()
            current_score = best_score
            s = 1.0

        if current_score + 1e-14 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_p = p.copy()

        if maxc <= 1e-300:
            break

        sigma = focus * maxc
        if sigma <= 1e-300:
            sigma = 1e-12
        z = (c - maxc) / sigma
        w = np.exp(z)
        wsum = float(np.sum(w))
        if (not np.isfinite(wsum)) or wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= wsum

        w_rev = w[::-1]
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, ffl)
        corr = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, ffl)
        idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n, dtype=int)
        grad = 2.0 * corr[idx]
        grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)

        d = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        pnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(p))

        if (not np.isfinite(dnorm)) or dnorm <= 1e-15 or (not np.isfinite(pnorm)) or pnorm <= 0.0:
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
            noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
            p = project_to_simplex(p + 1e-7 / float(n) * noise, 1.0)
            iters += 1
            continue

        move = (alpha * pnorm / dnorm) * d
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(move)):
            iters += 1
            continue
        max_move = float(np.max(np.abs(move)))
        if max_move > 5.0 * pnorm:
            move *= (5.0 * pnorm) / max_move

        accepted = False
        for scale in (1.6, 1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15):
            q = p - scale * move
            q = project_to_simplex(q, 1.0)
            sc = score_fft(q, ffl)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current_score - 1e-12:
                p = q
                current_score = sc
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            if current_score + 1e-14 < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            alpha = min(1.5, alpha * 1.05)
            focus = max(0.02, focus * 0.995)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            alpha = max(0.05, alpha * 0.85)
            focus = max(0.02, focus * 0.90)
            if no_improve > 100:
                noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
                noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
                p = project_to_simplex(
                    best_p + 0.2 * (float(np.linalg.norm(best_p)) / max(1.0, np.sqrt(n))) * noise,
                    1.0,
                )
                current_score = score_fft(p, ffl)
                if (not np.isfinite(current_score)) or current_score >= best_score:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    current_score = best_score
                no_improve = 0
                alpha = float(alpha0)
                focus = float(focus0)

        iters += 1
        if iters % 200 == 0:
            print(f"[{label}] iter={iters} current={current_score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f}")

    return best_p, best_score


def exact_score(seq):
    """Authoritative score using the supplied evaluate_sequence, if available."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).tolist()
        return float(evaluate_sequence(a))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main search routine. Returns a list of nonnegative heights."""
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 35.0)
    deadline = t_start + budget
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    M = 4

    candidates = []

    # 1) Low-dimensional coefficient exploration at medium length.
    coeff_start = time.monotonic()
    coeff_total = min(180.0, 0.14 * budget)
    coeff_deadline = coeff_start + coeff_total
    top = []

    # Try both midpoint and endpoint sampling; midpoint is usually smoother.
    for shift, frac in ((0.5, 0.65), (0.0, 0.35)):
        alloc = coeff_total * frac
        dl = coeff_start + alloc
        if dl <= time.monotonic():
            continue
        part = trig_random_search(512, M, float(shift), dl, rng, top_size=8)
        for sc, item in part:
            add_top(top, sc, item, 8)

    top.sort(key=_score_key)

    # 2) Refine best coefficients at n=1024.
    refine_deadline = min(deadline, coeff_deadline + min(120.0, 0.10 * budget))
    top = refine_top_coefficients(top, 1024, M, refine_deadline, rng, max_refine=3)
    if top:
        print(f"top coeff after refinement: {top[0][0]:.8f}")

    fallback_x = np.zeros(2 * M, dtype=float)
    fallback_x[0] = 0.086
    fallback_x[2] = -0.72
    if not top:
        top = [(2.0, (0.5, fallback_x.copy()))]

    # Optional previous construction, if present.
    extra_p = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)  # noqa: F821
        if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
            arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            s = float(np.sum(arr))
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                arr = arr / s
                extra_p = upsample_p(arr, 2048)
    except Exception:
        extra_p = None

    # 3) Full local search at n=2048 from the best trigonometric starts.
    n2 = 2048
    p_starts2 = []
    for _, item in top[:2]:
        shift, x = item
        C = make_trig_basis(n2, M, float(shift))
        p_starts2.append(coeff_to_p(x, n2, C))
    if len(p_starts2) < 2 and extra_p is not None:
        p_starts2.append(np.asarray(extra_p, dtype=float).copy())
    if not p_starts2:
        C = make_trig_basis(n2, M, 0.5)
        p_starts2.append(coeff_to_p(fallback_x, n2, C))

    local2_deadline = min(deadline - 220.0, refine_deadline + 0.55 * budget)
    best_2048_p = None
    best_2048_score = np.inf

    if local2_deadline > time.monotonic() + 60.0:
        alloc2 = local2_deadline - time.monotonic()
        seg2 = alloc2 / float(max(1, len(p_starts2)))
        for i, p0 in enumerate(p_starts2):
            dl = min(local2_deadline, time.monotonic() + seg2 * 1.15)
            if dl <= time.monotonic() + 20.0:
                break
            p1, s1 = local_search(p0, dl, rng, label=f"L2-{i}")
            if np.isfinite(s1):
                candidates.append((s1, p1))
                if s1 < best_2048_score:
                    best_2048_score = s1
                    best_2048_p = p1
            if time.monotonic() >= local2_deadline:
                break

    # 4) If time remains, refine to n=4096 from the best continuous start.
    n4 = 4096
    local4_deadline = deadline - 60.0
    if local4_deadline - time.monotonic() > 250.0:
        starts4 = []
        if top:
            shift, x = top[0][1]
            C4 = make_trig_basis(n4, M, float(shift))
            starts4.append(coeff_to_p(x, n4, C4))
        if best_2048_p is not None:
            starts4.append(upsample_p(best_2048_p, n4))
        elif extra_p is not None:
            starts4.append(upsample_p(extra_p, n4))
        else:
            C4 = make_trig_basis(n4, M, 0.5)
            starts4.append(coeff_to_p(fallback_x, n4, C4))

        ffl4 = next_power_of_two(3 * n4 - 2)
        best_start = None
        best_start_score = np.inf
        for ps in starts4:
            sc = score_fft(ps, ffl4)
            if sc < best_start_score:
                best_start_score = sc
                best_start = ps

        if best_start is not None:
            p1, s1 = local_search(best_start, local4_deadline, rng, label="L4")
            if np.isfinite(s1):
                candidates.append((s1, p1))

    # 5) Add initial sequences without local-search overhead as safety nets.
    if best_2048_p is not None:
        candidates.append((best_2048_score, best_2048_p.copy()))
    if top:
        for _, item in top[:3]:
            shift, x = item
            sn = 1024
            C = make_trig_basis(sn, M, float(shift))
            p = coeff_to_p(x, sn, C)
            sc = score_fft(p, next_power_of_two(3 * sn - 2))
            candidates.append((sc, p))

    if not candidates:
        candidates.append((2.0, normalize_sequence(np.ones(1024, dtype=float))))

    candidates.sort(key=_score_key)
    if candidates:
        print(f"best custom score before final exact check: {candidates[0][0]:.8f}")

    # 6) Use the authoritative evaluator sparingly to choose final sequence.
    best_seq = candidates[0][1]
    best_exact = np.inf
    exact_calls = 0
    for _, seq in candidates:
        if exact_calls >= 4:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        if seq.size > 4096 and exact_calls >= 2:
            break
        est = exact_score(seq)
        exact_calls += 1
        if np.isfinite(est) and est < best_exact:
            best_exact = est
            best_seq = seq
            print(f"exact best so far: {est:.8f}, n={seq.size}")

    final_seq = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=float))
    final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
